**[00:21:25.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000379)  
we also track kung ilan po yung units ng nagpapatakbo ng mass transportation system natin

**[00:21:32.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000380)  
before modernization started a decade ago.

**[00:21:35.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000381)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:21:37.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000382)  
So, this is from CIDS, UPCIDS.

**[00:21:41.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000383)  
According to their data, there used to be 300,000 PUJ units operating.

**[00:21:49.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000384)  
And kung total PUJs operating as of August 2025 was 128,000 plus,

**[00:21:55.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000385)  
minus po natin siya, around 171,000 PUJ units were lost due to modernization.

**[00:22:04.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000386)  
Yung mahigit 170,000 na na-phase out na PUJs,

**[00:22:08.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000387)  
matumbas po niyan if you include yung, or if you count yung average passenger capacity of 20,

**[00:22:15.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000388)  
halos 3.5 million na passenger capacity ang nawasak.

**[00:22:19.140] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000389)  
Tapos using 10 daily trips, parang 5 round trips po yan, isa, dalawa, ganun.

**[00:22:24.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000390)  
That's 34 million daily passenger capacity niyo nawala dahil sa na-phase out po na 170,000 PUJs.

**[00:22:33.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000391)  
So, ang tanong ko po, Mr. Chair,

**[00:22:36.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000392)  
napunan po ba ng PTMP itong kawalan ng daily passenger capacity nung tinulak na magkaroon ng modern jeepneys?

**[00:22:46.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000393)  
Na according to the data here also shown, na may average capacity niyo, sabihin na po natin 25 katao.

**[00:22:51.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000394)  
Pero dahil 12,000 units lang,

**[00:22:54.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000395)  
as of 2025, or 200, or nearing 300,000 ang passenger capacity and 3 million daily passenger capacity yung naiambag.

**[00:23:05.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000396)  
So, yun po ang tanong natin, Mr. Chair, napunan po ba yung nawalang 34 million daily passenger capacity ng PTMP?

**[00:23:14.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000397)  
Mr. Chair, with your permission po, I'm quite sure po nasama yan sa ating pag-aaral sa PTMP,

**[00:23:19.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000398)  
but I will defer po to ASEC Dioscoro Reyes po, yung in-charge po ngayon,

**[00:23:24.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000399)  
ng road and infra natin.

**[00:23:33.850] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000400)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair.

**[00:23:36.370] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000401)  
Ma'am Renee, good afternoon.

**[00:23:38.830] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000402)  
Ma'am, yung po, ano, yung sinasabi natin nakakulangan ng sasakyan

**[00:23:43.470] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000403)  
because of the continuing growth of private vehicle use.

**[00:23:51.310] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000404)  
Mr. Chair, may I request the resource person to speak closer to the mic? Thank you.

**[00:23:56.330] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000405)  
Yung po nga traffic congestion natin,

**[00:24:00.710] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000406)  
yung mga naranasan natin,

**[00:24:02.050] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000407)  
yung mga naranasan natin na paghihirap ng ating mga nanatanggap,

**[00:24:05.050] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000408)  
ay because of the continuing growth of private vehicle use,

**[00:24:09.870] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000409)  
further increasing pressure on limited road space,

**[00:24:14.190] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000410)  
particularly where public transport is perceived as inconvenient, unreliable, inaccessible, or poorly integrated.

**[00:24:22.170] DOTr Assistant Secretary Dioscoro T. Reyes** (asr-000411)  
Therefore, traffic congestion cannot be addressed solely by increasing road capacity or contracting [unclear].

**[00:24:30.090] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000412)  
Mr. Chair,

**[00:24:32.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000413)  
but the answer po pertains to traffic.

**[00:24:35.650] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000414)  
Ang question po natin is yung daily passenger capacity.

**[00:24:39.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000415)  
In short po, given this data,

**[00:24:41.570] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000416)  
kumonti po yung units of mass transportation system na kayang mag-accommodate ng passengers.

**[00:24:48.370] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000417)  
Sabihin natin, factor yung traffic sa public transit rating natin na mababa.

**[00:24:53.710] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000418)  
Pero isang bahagi lang po yun.

**[00:24:55.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000419)  
Ang isang bahagi din po is kumonti po talaga yung vehicles na according to the numbers of the ORTR mismo,

**[00:25:02.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000420)  
hindi po napunan yung natanggal na 170,000 PUJs.

**[00:25:06.850] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000421)  
So, yan po ang tanong po natin.

**[00:25:08.890] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000422)  
Paano na po yung mass transportation system natin na ganitong natanggal,

**[00:25:13.470] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000423)  
and nakikita, at nararamdaman ng maamayan yung kahirapan?

**[00:25:17.150] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000424)  
Mr. Chair?

**[00:25:18.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000425)  
Mr. Chair, if I may answer po.

**[00:25:20.770] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000426)  
Tama po.

**[00:25:21.650] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000427)  
Yung ating modernization program kasi po,

**[00:25:24.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000428)  
ang pangunahing layunin dyan is yung roadworthiness din po ng ating mga lumang PUVs.

**[00:25:31.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000429)  
Ito rin po ay isa.

**[00:25:32.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000430)  
Sa mga dahilan, kung sa tingin po natin, kumonti yung mga existing PUVs natin.

**[00:25:38.710] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000431)  
Pero yan po yung sinasabi natin, dapat magkaroon din tayo ng tamang route rationalization.

**[00:25:44.710] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000432)  
Kasi, admittedly, inaayos pa yan ng DOTR, kasi po talaga naging problema ho yan ng ating PTMP.

**[00:25:52.890] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000433)  
Ganun pa man, pilit namin pinupunuhan po yung pagkukulang ng per route.

**[00:25:58.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000434)  
At ito'y magagawa lang namin kapag may tama kaming pakikipag-ugnayan

**[00:26:02.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000435)  
sa local

**[00:26:02.710] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000436)  
government.

**[00:26:04.290] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000437)  
For example po, sa tingin namin, noong last year po,

**[00:26:07.770] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000438)  
pinakita nyo pong letrato, I think that's a long Commonwealth Avenue.

**[00:26:12.390] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000439)  
Noong tayo po yung mismong nag-commute, nakita natin na kulang nga yung mga PUVs dyan.

**[00:26:19.250] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000440)  
At kaya nagsabi tayo sa LTFRB na kailangan nyo magdagdag.

**[00:26:23.330] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000441)  
Kailangan nyo ayusin yung ating mga ruta.

**[00:26:26.390] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000442)  
At para mas mabigyan natin ng tamang number of units na babaybay sa isang.

**[00:26:32.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000443)  
So the answer for this decrease of the number is not basically po nawalan sila ng sasakyan.

**[00:26:42.250] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000444)  
More of nag-modernize po yung iba.

**[00:26:45.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000445)  
May route rationalization na po kung anong talagang number of units na tama

**[00:26:48.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000446)  
at number of authorized units na bumabaybay.

**[00:26:51.830] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000447)  
At pangatlo po yung mga natanggal po natin na mga hindi na po roadworthy na mga lumang PUVs po.

**[00:26:59.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000448)  
Mr. Chair, my next question po.

**[00:27:02.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000449)  
Mag-next slide.

**[00:27:03.870] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000450)  
This is data po from the DOTR website.

**[00:27:06.910] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000451)  
Nakapansin-pansin po na since napondohan ang PTMP from 2018,

**[00:27:11.850] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000452)  
totaling nearly 9 billion pesos.

**[00:27:14.710] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000453)  
Kalakhan po ng pondo nito ay unobligated.

**[00:27:17.530] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000454)  
So kung titignan po natin yung numbers,

**[00:27:21.150] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000455)  
ito po ay galing po sa DOTR road sector data sets po from the website.

**[00:27:26.410] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000456)  
Ayun po, may mga ilang years na unobligated yung nilaan ng GAA.

**[00:27:32.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000457)  
200 million or even yung parang 1 billion pesos for supposed to be sa PTMP.

**[00:27:39.650] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000458)  
Hindi rin po reflected kasi sa website kung nagastos po ba yung fund.

**[00:27:44.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000459)  
For example, 2018 up to 2019 dahil meron nga po 2 years na essential shelf life yung budget.

**[00:27:51.470] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000460)  
Pwede po bang makahingi ng explanation from DOTR on this?

**[00:27:54.970] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-000461)  
Cong. Renee, please wrap up your question.

**[00:27:56.770] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000462)

**[00:27:58.370] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000464)

Kasi ngayon po we downloaded,

**[00:28:21.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000469)  
yung nag-ayos na po kami ng 1.5 billion na i-download po namin sa LTFRB.

**[00:28:27.970] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000470)  
Ito po yung binabanggit ko po kanina.

**[00:28:29.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000471)  
Nang the DOTR together with the LTFRB, pati yung PTMP po namin na sektor,

**[00:28:36.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000472)

**[00:28:41.210] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000473)

**[00:28:45.630] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000474)

**[00:28:51.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000475)

**[00:28:53.790] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000476)  
So that 1.5 billion po na sinasabi, including the continuing budget,

**[00:28:59.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000477)  
of 2025, tama po, may continuing budget,

**[00:29:03.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000478)  
kaya may naiwan po yata ang 400 plus million.

**[00:29:06.910] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000479)  
Yun po yung actual number po dyan.

**[00:29:09.370] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000480)  
Ay i-download po namin sa LTFRB upang mabigyan natin ng amortization tulong po

**[00:29:16.430] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000481)

**[00:29:22.970] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000482)  
So yan po, we can update that po.

**[00:29:25.850] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000483)  
Nakipagtulungan po kami sa DBM po dun sa SARO.

**[00:29:29.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000484)  
At meron lang kaming bibigay ng mga dokumento para po ay mailabas na po yung SARO po.

**[00:50:12.280] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000968)

**[00:50:18.000] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000969)

**[00:50:23.200] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000970)  
which initially, Marina provided po yung mga libre training para sa trainers natin.

**[00:50:29.120] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000971)  
But the National Maritime Polytechnic is providing po yung...

**[00:50:34.460] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000972)  
meron po silang mga free training for our seafarers.

**[00:50:38.020] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000973)

**[00:50:43.080] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000974)

**[00:50:48.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000975)

**[00:50:53.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000976)

**[00:50:57.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000977)

**[00:51:04.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000978)

**[00:51:07.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000979)

**[00:51:11.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000980)  
o masagot sa pamamagitan, halimbawa, ng TESDA or CHED

**[00:51:15.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000981)  
o maaari pang convergence program or partnership

**[00:51:19.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000982)  
para matiyak din yung access sa ganitong tipo ng mga kurso.

**[00:51:25.370] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000983)  
Mr. Chair, if I may, we are planning to provide free training

**[00:51:29.890] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000984)  
dito po sa mga paparating na bagong requirements

**[00:51:33.070] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000985)  
like the generic...

**[00:51:36.450] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000986)  
courses for to address automation and decarbonization.

**[00:51:41.450] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000987)

**[00:51:46.950] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000988)

**[00:51:50.690] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000989)  
in preparation for the mandatory requirements to be imposed

**[00:51:54.650] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000990)  
by the International Maritime Organization.

**[00:51:56.650] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-000991)  
Mr. Chair, thank you po.

**[00:59:05.420] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001159)  
Representative Elago.

**[00:59:07.720] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001160)  
Next to interpellate is

**[00:59:09.240] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-001161)  
Representative Claudine Bautista-Lim.

**[00:59:13.400] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001162)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:59:14.760] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001163)  
Thank you,

**[00:59:16.440] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001164)  
dear colleagues, and good afternoon

**[00:59:18.200] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001165)  
dear colleagues and to the DOTr

**[00:59:20.600] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001166)  
family headed by

**[00:59:22.200] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001167)  
Sec. Banoy Lopez.

**[00:59:24.620] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001168)  
I have several points

**[00:59:26.840] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001169)  
that I am concerned with

**[00:59:28.840] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001170)  
with regard to your budget.

**[00:59:30.720] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001171)  
Let me start off with the

**[00:59:32.720] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001172)  
EDSA busway.

**[00:59:34.160] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001173)  
As we know by now, there is zero

**[00:59:36.740] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001174)  
allocation for the EDSA busway.

**[00:59:40.720] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001175)  
I think this is the first time

**[00:59:42.600] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001176)  
that the EDSA busway has been defunded

**[00:59:44.700] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001177)  
and there is no line item

**[00:59:46.060] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001178)  
for the 2027

**[00:59:47.900] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001179)  
NEP.

**[00:59:50.340] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001180)  
So,

**[00:59:52.120] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001181)  
I know that the DOTR

**[00:59:55.240] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001182)  
provides funding

**[00:59:56.360] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001183)  
for the operation

**[00:59:57.520] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001184)  
and

**[00:59:58.900] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001185)  
the

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001196)  
maintenance of the EDSA busway.

**[01:00:03.540] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001197)  
Alam po natin na may mga taong ini-employ si DOTR

**[01:00:07.760] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001198)  
para to make sure that these stations are well-maintained,

**[01:00:12.300] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001199)  
are safe, are accessible, are clean.

**[01:00:18.440] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001200)  
And alam po natin na madaming renovations na ginagawa din sa EDSA busway

**[01:00:23.960] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001201)  
sa mga stations natin.

**[01:00:25.520] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001202)  
And dyan din po of course ang saig na we're making sure that the passengers are also safe

**[01:00:33.420] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001203)  
because they are the first responders in case of any untoward incident.

**[01:00:39.640] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001204)  
So my question is, what is the impact of the defunding of the EDSA busway?

**[01:00:46.720] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001205)  
Ano po ang mga services or projects, programs ng DOTR ang mahihinto

**[01:00:54.940] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001206)  
sa EDSA busway?

**[01:00:55.520] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001207)  
At madi-disrupt nang dahil wala po kayong pondo?

**[01:01:02.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001208)  
With the permission, Mr. Chair, may I defer to Senior Undersecretary Pastor?

**[01:01:07.060] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001209)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[01:01:10.570] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001210)  
And through the Chair,

**[01:01:13.390] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001211)  
tama po, Mr. Chair, marami pong mawawalang serbisyo

**[01:01:17.450] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001212)  
ang ating pong EDSA busway kung hindi po mapupondohan for 2027.

**[01:01:23.750] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001213)  
Unahin ko po ang maintenance po natin.

**[01:01:27.490] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001214)  
As we speak, meron po tayo,

**[01:01:29.330] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001215)  
yung service provider na naninigurado po na malinis po ang ating busway

**[01:01:34.430] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001216)  
at nandito na rin po yung maintenance ng ating mga manlips and elevators

**[01:01:38.730] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001217)  
para po sa mga PWD natin upang masigurado po na magagamit po ito

**[01:01:45.970] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001218)  
ng ating mga kababayan na may kapansanan.

**[01:01:48.590] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001219)  
Pangalawa po ay yung siguridad ng ating mga pasahero

**[01:01:51.630] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001220)  
dahil katulad po ng nabanggit ninyo,

**[01:01:55.090] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001221)  
yung ating saik at ganyan din po yung security services natin

**[01:01:58.450] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001222)  
ay nakaangkla po

**[01:01:59.330] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001223)  
ang kanilang kontrata at mga sahod

**[01:02:02.490] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001224)  
dito po sa ating EDSA busway project management office.

**[01:02:08.150] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001225)  
Ngunit ang pinakamabigat po dito ay yung ating kasalukuyang proyekto

**[01:02:14.650] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001226)  
na nirehabilitate po natin lahat ng istasyon ng ating busway.

**[01:02:19.130] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001227)  
Sa kasalukuyan po, kita po natin yung improvement

**[01:02:23.210] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001228)  
ng kamuning busway station po natin.

**[01:02:26.330] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001229)  
I wanted to highlight this kasi po,

**[01:02:29.330] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001230)  
yung rehabilitation niya,

**[01:02:31.150] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001231)  
sa isang buwan po, 90,000 ang ating ridership

**[01:02:33.790] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001232)  
for that particular station.

**[01:02:36.010] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001233)  
Pero nung na-renovate po ito,

**[01:02:38.110] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001234)  
nagkaroon ng manlift, nagkaroon ng elevator,

**[01:02:40.850] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001235)  
nagkaroon po ng mga upuan sa ating bus station,

**[01:02:43.650] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001236)  
tumaas po ang ating ridership for this particular station

**[01:02:46.890] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001237)  
to 190,000 passengers per month.

**[01:02:51.110] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001238)  
More than 100% po yung tinaas

**[01:02:52.970] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001239)  
at ito po ay patunay na talagang malaki po ang bagay

**[01:02:57.050] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001240)  
ang rehabilitasyon ng ating EDSA busway.

**[01:02:59.330] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001241)  
As we speak po, meron po tayong apat na istasyon

**[01:03:03.390] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001242)  
na nirehabilitate at tatlong istasyon na kailangan po,

**[01:03:08.210] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001243)  
tatlong bagong istasyon po.

**[01:03:10.150] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001244)  
So for 2027, Mr. Chair, Honorable Bautista Lim,

**[01:03:15.790] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001245)  
kami po ay humingi ng pondong 1.7 billion

**[01:03:18.370] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001246)  
to rehabilitate 12 more stations

**[01:03:20.450] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001247)  
at magiging hawig-hawig na po lahat ng ating mga istasyon

**[01:03:23.830] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001248)  
pati yung mga facilities natin

**[01:03:25.350] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001249)  
kung kaya't kami po ay dumudulog at humingi po ng tulong

**[01:03:28.890] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001250)  
sa ating House of Representatives

**[01:03:32.190] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001251)  
upang magkaroon po kami ng pondo sana next year

**[01:03:35.070] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001252)  
para matuloy po ito.

**[01:03:36.170] DOTr Undersecretary Mark Steven C. Pastor** (asr-001253)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[01:03:38.070] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001254)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:03:39.550] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001255)  
So malaki po talaga ang impact ng pagdidefund

**[01:03:44.530] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001256)  
ng ating EDSA busway.

**[01:03:46.410] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001257)  
So I am appealing to my colleagues here in Congress

**[01:03:51.230] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001258)  
and the Committee on Appropriations

**[01:03:53.730] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001259)  
that maybe we can give the requested amount of 1.7 billion

**[01:03:58.890] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001260)  
to support for the maintenance

**[01:04:02.710] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001261)  
and the operations of the EDSA busway

**[01:04:06.550] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001262)  
because nakita po natin, [unclear] po natin

**[01:04:09.870] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001263)  
ano ang magiging kawalan nito

**[01:04:11.710] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001264)  
at ano ang magiging impact nito sa ating mananakay.

**[01:04:17.810] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001265)  
Thank you for that, Mr. Yusek Steve

**[01:04:20.470] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001266)  
and thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:21.790] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001267)  
For my second point,

**[01:04:23.970] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001268)  
for the fuel subsidy,

**[01:04:26.070] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001269)  
zero budget din po tayo

**[01:04:28.890] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001270)

**[01:04:31.810] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001271)  
I know for a fact that 2025,

**[01:04:34.350] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001272)  
we have 2.5 billion for the fuel subsidy.

**[01:04:38.090] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001273)  
2026, we have zero budget again

**[01:04:40.990] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001274)

**[01:04:44.370] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001275)  
Now, nung oil crisis this year,

**[01:04:49.770] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001277)

**[01:04:54.030] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001278)

Ang 2.5 billion allocation

**[01:04:59.890] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001281)

**[01:05:03.830] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001282)  
Tama po kayo dun, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:05:25.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001291)  
ay fully disbursed na po.

**[01:05:29.060] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001292)  
So,

**[01:05:29.400] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001293)  
I think that's a good point.

**[01:05:30.260] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001294)  
You mean to say wala na kayong matatap

**[01:05:32.760] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001295)  
in case po, Mr. Secretary,

**[01:05:36.120] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001296)

**[01:05:38.420] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001297)

**[01:05:39.720] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001298)

**[01:05:43.780] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001299)  
so wala na po tayong budget na magagamit

**[01:05:46.940] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001300)  
para tulungan po ang ating mga

**[01:05:49.180] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001301)

**[01:05:53.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001302)  
Sa amin pong allotment po,

**[01:05:55.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001303)

**[01:05:58.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001305)  
ay andyan pa rin po ang

**[01:06:03.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001309)

**[01:06:04.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001310)

**[01:06:11.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001315)  
para ng pondo

**[01:06:12.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001316)

**[01:06:14.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001317)  
I should say,

**[01:06:15.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001318)  
fuel subsidy po.

**[01:06:17.320] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001319)  
Maraming salamat,

**[01:06:19.080] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001320)  
Sec.

**[01:06:19.400] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001321)  
So, magkano po yung nirequest

**[01:06:21.240] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001322)  
na fuel subsidy sana

**[01:06:22.660] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001323)

**[01:06:26.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001324)  
If I'm not mistaken,

**[01:06:28.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001325)  
5 billion din po.

**[01:06:29.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001326)  
5 billion.

**[01:06:30.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001327)  
Yes.

**[01:06:31.540] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001328)  
Okay, because I'm just

**[01:06:34.780] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001329)

and drivers. So,

I am appealing

na magkaroon po

ng 5 billion

**[01:07:13.520] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001351)  
budget

**[01:07:14.680] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001352)  
for the fuel subsidy

**[01:07:16.760] Rep. Claudine Diana D. Bautista-Lim** (asr-001353)  
in case,

magkaroon na naman

**[02:00:00.000] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003392)  
Sir, only two concerns. We wanted that if there was a way that we'll be able to change the airport ID from Cagayan de Oro to Lagindingan, as to give way and honor Lagindingan and the province of Misamis Oriental. Would that be possible, Sir?

**[02:00:19.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003393)  
Yan po yung pag-uusapan natin sa CAAP. Kasi alam ko po may regulasyon po yan pagdating sa IK1 natin, pagdating sa mga paglalagay ng tamang pangalan at saka code po. Sige po, isasama ko po kagad po sa aming pagpupulong po.

**[02:00:40.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003394)  
And if it would be too much of a challenge, Sir, maybe ganito na lang. Welcome to Lagindingan Airport, Gateway to Cagayan de Oro or Northern Mindanao.

**[02:00:49.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003395)  
I think we can do that, Sir.

**[02:00:53.320] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003396)  
It would be very challenging for them to change the airport ID because I've been asking them for so long already, Sir. At least to give honor to Lagindingan, the host of the airport, Sir.

**[02:01:05.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003397)  
To advise CAAP, as a co-grantor also of Lagindingan Airport, to advise the proponent po to make sure po na mayroong due recognition to the Cagayan de Oro.

**[02:01:16.580] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003398)  
That would be of great help.

**[02:01:18.820] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003399)  
And of course,

**[02:01:19.720] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003400)  
promotion to the province of Misamis, Oriental, Sir. Thank you.

**[02:01:22.940] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003401)  
Second, Sir.

Mr. Chairman,

Mr. Chairman, short interjection lang.

**[02:01:27.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-003404)

**[02:01:29.400] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-003405)

**[02:01:32.300] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-003406)

**[02:01:33.720] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-003407)  
ilagay yung picture nung nandoon eh.

**[02:01:35.740] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-003408)  
Pwede. Pwede.

**[02:01:37.500] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003409)  
Laging na lang natin doon sa airport, sa aeroplano

**[02:01:40.020] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003410)  
ng Cebu Pacific, PAL, at saka

**[02:01:41.660] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003411)  
iba pa. And that's my second

**[02:01:43.840] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003412)  
and second to the last, Mr. Chair.

contract was awarded the

**[02:01:49.360] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003415)

**[02:01:51.320] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003416)

**[02:01:53.900] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003417)

**[02:12:32.850] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003698)

**[02:12:35.250] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003699)

**[02:12:37.110] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003700)

**[02:12:39.270] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003701)

**[02:12:41.490] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003702)

**[02:12:43.350] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003703)

**[02:12:45.610] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003704)

**[02:12:55.750] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003709)

**[02:12:56.970] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003710)

**[02:12:59.230] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003711)

**[02:13:01.490] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003712)  
Meron pa po,

**[02:13:02.850] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003713)  
pero at least for yung mga sinasabi natin

**[02:13:05.150] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003714)

**[02:13:07.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003715)  
for example, yung N1 po

**[02:13:08.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003716)

**[02:13:10.890] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003717)  
ay almost 90% na po yung right of way

**[02:13:13.410] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003718)  
natin. Pero isang segment lang po

**[02:13:15.390] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003719)  
kasi yan. Yung Subway naman natin,

**[02:13:17.370] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003720)  
90% na po yung right of way

**[02:13:19.090] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003721)  
acquisition natin. Pero yung pagdating

**[02:13:21.330] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003722)  
sa kabuuan ng NSCR

**[02:13:23.510] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003723)  
natin, ay kulang

**[02:13:25.350] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003724)  
pa po yung pondo ng right of way

**[02:13:27.230] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003725)  
namin. Kasi kapag pagkukuhanan

**[02:13:29.350] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003726)  
lang po namin is the GOP

**[02:13:31.270] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003727)  
kung saan maaari kami kumuha ng right of way

**[02:13:33.690] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003728)  
funding. Iyan po ay dapat

**[02:13:35.610] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003729)  
nakalaan din sa pagbayad namin

**[02:13:37.510] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003730)  
ng mga VAT and IT other

**[02:13:39.290] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003731)  
financing charges. Kaya tama po

**[02:13:41.630] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003732)  
kayo, kailangan namin ng right of way

**[02:13:43.590] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003733)  
kasi ang nakikita lang po natin

**[02:13:45.450] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003734)  
yung Ciriles, pero kailangan namin

**[02:13:47.530] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003735)  
ng pondo ng right of way para sa

**[02:13:49.530] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003736)  
mga sektor po ng

**[02:13:51.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003737)  
aviation, maritime at road din po.

**[02:13:55.350] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003738)  
Now, kunyari, for fiscal year,

**[02:13:57.170] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003739)  
talking about the north-south commuter

**[02:13:59.530] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003740)  
railway system, for

**[02:14:01.270] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003741)  
2026, it was

**[02:14:03.330] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003742)  
100, almost, a little

**[02:14:05.490] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003743)  
above 123 billion.

**[02:14:08.170] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003744)  
Ano na ba yung

**[02:14:08.830] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003745)  
appropriated, obligated, at

**[02:14:11.530] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003746)

**[02:14:27.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003747)  
With your permission, po, Mr. Chair,

**[02:14:29.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003748)  
I'll defer po to Usec TJ.

**[02:14:31.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003749)  
You should have his number, po, pagdating

**[02:14:33.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003750)  
sa obligation and utilization.

**[02:14:36.260] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003751)  
Mr. Chair, for

**[02:14:37.620] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003752)  
NSCR

**[02:14:38.400] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003753)  
2026, and

**[02:14:40.760] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003754)  
2026, continuing,

**[02:14:44.160] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003755)  
2025, continuing,

**[02:14:45.160] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003756)  
total po is

**[02:14:47.500] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003757)  
67 billion.

**[02:14:49.700] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003758)  
And then as of June

**[02:14:51.000] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003759)  
2026, obligation

**[02:14:53.180] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003760)  
is at 89.89%,

**[02:14:56.780] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003761)  
and

**[02:14:57.780] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003762)  
disbursement is at

**[02:14:58.920] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003763)  
47.9%.

**[02:15:00.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003764)  
And now in your estimation, to complete the project, sapat na ba yung alokasyon mo?

**[02:15:08.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003765)  
Ilan pa yung kukulangin? Ilan pa yung kakailanganin?

**[02:15:13.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003766)  
I'll defer to you, Sec. TJ, kasi yung budget proposal po namin,

**[02:15:17.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003767)  
ay malaki po yung variance pagdating po sa NEP na binigay sa dalawang proyektong ito.

**[02:15:21.960] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003768)  
So, Mr. Chair, kulang po. Ang original po na budget proposal ni NSCR for 2027 was P313 billion.

**[02:15:33.880] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003769)  
And we were given po, as you have noted, P100.

**[02:15:38.960] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003770)  
So, may additional funding requirements po that will still be rolled over to the following [unclear].

**[02:15:44.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003771)  
But dito sa P312 billion na kailangan nyo,

**[02:15:50.460] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003772)  
is it a portion of...

**[02:15:51.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003773)  
is also financed, sourced from foreign-funded loans?

**[02:15:58.800] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003774)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Out of this P313 billion, P265 billion is coming from the NJICA loans.

**[02:16:08.420] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003775)  
Okay. Kung may P261 billion ka coming from loans,

**[02:16:12.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003776)  
and then P123 galing sa appropriations ng Congress,

**[02:16:17.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003777)  
bakit kukulangin ka pa rin if you only need P300 billion?

**[02:16:21.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003778)  
Plus.

**[02:16:22.760] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003779)  
Mr. Chair, out of the P265 billion loan proceeds needed,

**[02:16:28.920] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003780)  
only P97 billion in the NEP.

**[02:16:32.260] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003781)  
And out of the P47 billion GOP counterpart needed,

**[02:16:37.200] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-003782)  
only P26 billion is in the NEP.

**[02:16:39.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003783)  
Okay. So, yun nga yung actually yung nagiging concern ko ulit.

**[02:16:49.480] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003784)  
Because we undertake projects which...

**[02:16:53.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003785)  
which are not fully funded.

**[02:16:56.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003786)  
Ang problema, pagka nangutang ka,

**[02:16:59.060] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003787)  
nagbabayad ka ng interest sa utang,

**[02:17:02.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003788)  
pero hindi umuusad yung proyekyo.

**[02:17:05.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003789)  
We end up losing a bigger portion of the limited fiscal funds available to us.

**[02:17:17.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003790)  
So, well, given these challenges,

**[02:17:20.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003791)  
yung north to south, kailan po natin matatapos?

**[02:17:23.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003792)  
Ang target?

**[02:17:23.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003793)  
Ang target natin?

**[02:17:25.480] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003794)  
The N1 po, yung malolos hanggang Valenzuela,

**[02:17:28.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003795)  
the first phase po ay target natin.

**[02:17:30.900] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003796)  
2027?

**[02:17:31.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003797)  
December.

**[02:17:33.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003798)  
Pero this goes all the way to Clark, diba?

**[02:17:35.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003799)  
Yes po.

**[02:17:36.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003800)  
Yung pagdating po natin hanggang sa Clark po,

**[02:17:39.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003801)  
the entire Valenzuela,

**[02:17:41.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003802)  
ang target po namin for the demonstration run po,

**[02:17:44.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003803)  
ibig sabihin hindi siya revenue operations,

**[02:17:46.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003804)  
yung N2 po natin ay sana po matapos namin

**[02:17:49.960] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003805)  
yung demonstration run by June of 2028 po.

**[02:17:53.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003806)  
Tapos after that, doon na po kasi yung tamang testing and commissioning

**[02:17:57.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003807)  
at para sa revenue operations na po.

**[02:17:59.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003808)  
So, for revenue operations, kailan ang ano natin? Estimated?

**[02:18:02.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003809)  
I think our estimate is more or less last quarter of 2028.

**[02:18:08.180] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003810)  
Baka kakayanin na po.

**[02:18:09.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003811)  
Sino ba gumagawa nito?

**[02:18:11.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003812)  
It's composed of different packages po.

**[02:18:15.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003813)  
Contract packages.

**[02:18:16.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003814)  
And several contract packages have different contractors po.

**[02:18:22.320] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003815)  
Foreign contractors?

**[02:18:22.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003816)  
Foreign contractors, local or kasi ba?

**[02:18:25.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003817)  
May foreign contractors, but may ka-JV po na mga local contractors.

**[02:18:31.480] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003818)  
If I can name some po, ka-JV din po nila ang DMCI,

**[02:18:35.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003819)  
I think EEI.

**[02:18:38.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003820)  
These are reputable companies.

**[02:18:39.440] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003821)  
These are the big reputable local counterparts,

**[02:18:42.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003822)  
hindi yung kunsino-sino na lang.

**[02:18:44.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003823)  
Tama po, reputable Filipino counterparts po na companies po.

**[02:18:48.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003824)  
Now, what about the subway?

**[02:18:49.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003825)  
The subway, ganun din po.

**[02:18:51.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003826)  
It's a JICA.

**[02:18:52.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003827)  
Ang target natin sa subway is, I think, 2031.

**[02:18:59.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003828)  
Pero ang kagandahan po dito, kung masasabi ko lang po,

**[02:19:02.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003829)  
itong dito parte sa Mayquezon City,

**[02:19:05.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003830)  
yung East Valenzuela hanggang dito sa Quirino Station,

**[02:19:10.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003831)  
yung tunnel po niyan, both southbound and northbound,

**[02:19:14.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003832)  
100% na po.

**[02:19:16.460] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003833)  
This is, I think, 1.5 kilometers.

**[02:19:18.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003834)  
Buong-buo na po.

**[02:19:19.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003835)  
Butas na po yan.

**[02:19:20.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003836)  
Ganun din po yung ating North

**[02:19:22.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003837)  
Avenue hanggang Tandang Sora ho yata, 100% na rin po 'yan.

**[02:19:27.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003838)  
Tuloy-tuloy.

**[02:19:28.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003839)  
Then yung ating Camp Aguinaldo, papuntang Anona sa Casio,

**[02:19:31.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003840)  
I think around 90% na po yung tunnel natin dyan.

**[02:19:34.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003841)  
So talagang yung butas, kung hindi man 100%,

**[02:19:39.080] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003842)  
there's a significant progress po sa pagtatunnel natin sa mga ganitong subway po.

**[02:19:43.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003843)  
And what is the target date of completion?

**[02:19:47.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003844)  
Operation.

**[02:19:48.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003845)  
Operation is around 2031 po.

**[02:19:51.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003846)  
2031.

**[02:19:52.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003847)  
Lastly, dahil pinapahinto na ako,

**[02:19:54.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003848)  
yung concern lang dito sa ginagawa natin sa may line that runs through itong Commonwealth Avenue.

**[02:20:03.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003849)  
Meron tayong, I think, there's a terminal that will be placed sa may papasok ng University Avenue,

**[02:20:15.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003850)  
papasok ng UP doon sa labas.

**[02:20:17.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003851)  
I think this is kung saan malapit, almost katapat ng University Mall,

**[02:20:22.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003852)  
but yung initial plan was ang entrance and exit was nasa side lang ng UP

**[02:20:35.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003853)  
at wala doon sa kabilang side ng Commonwealth.

**[02:20:39.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003854)  
That was the initial plans that I think that was presented.

**[02:20:42.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003855)  
At ang concern was why couldn't it be on both sides?

**[02:20:49.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003856)  
Tapos I think UP raised the concern kasi ang initial plans was yung,

**[02:20:54.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003857)  
elevated yung terminal at hindi underground kasi yung that line goes under the Quezon Memorial Circle

**[02:21:07.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003858)  
then pumupunta sa, yun nga, lalabas sa Commonwealth, bakit kailangan sa taas yung...

**[02:21:15.880] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003859)  
If I may answer po, we're referring to the MRT-7 po, University Station.

**[02:21:23.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003860)  
I think a few months back.

**[02:21:26.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003861)  
We had a meeting with the UP also po, okay, with the President, Atty. Jimenez,

**[02:21:31.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003862)  
at nagpapasalamat kami at nagkasundo na po kami.

**[02:21:34.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003863)  
Una po, to address the situation, dalawa po, northbound and southbound.

**[02:21:38.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003864)  
Bali eh, hindi po, eastbound and westbound po.

**[02:21:42.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003865)  
Yung isa po ay bababa sa tingin namin doon sa may techno hub

**[02:21:46.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003866)  
para we can cover as many commuters as possible.

**[02:21:49.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003867)  
Yung isa po, yung papuntang westbound, ay the eastbound, bababa naman doon yung sa University Mall.

**[02:21:56.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003869)  
Yung isa po yung napakasunduan namin.

**[02:21:58.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003870)  
And it's elevated po.

**[02:21:59.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003871)  
At kung anong design na gusto po ng UP, ay susundan po namin.

**[02:22:04.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003872)  
Isang rason kung bakit po hindi kami pumayag na maging underground.

**[02:22:09.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003873)  
Ang Commonwealth po, napaka-traffic.

**[02:22:11.900] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003874)  
Imagine nyo po yan kapag ginawa nating underground,

**[02:22:14.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003875)  
and we're trying to hit the target po na 2027,

**[02:22:17.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003876)  
paano po yung cut and cover natin dyan?

**[02:22:21.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003877)  
We're just saying na, paano po, we're going to stop traffic doon sa UP.

**[02:22:26.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003878)  
Because we want to do it underground.

**[02:22:28.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003879)  
So I think the most practical way of addressing this is to elevate it.

**[02:22:32.180] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003880)  
Since elevated naman po lahat, halos,

**[02:22:34.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003881)  
and we'll make sure that the design as wanted by UP, we'll just adopt it.

**[02:22:39.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003882)  
Okay. So at least yun yung importante para sa akin,

**[02:22:42.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003883)

at he wanted to make sure that it goes out both sides.

Opo. Salamat po.

**[02:30:56.780] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-003891)  
Good afternoon. Next to interpolate is Congresswoman Anna York-Bondoc.

**[02:31:01.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003892)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chairs and members of the DOTR family.

**[02:31:10.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003893)  
Honorable Secretary, magandang hapon po sa inyong lahat.

**[02:31:15.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003894)  
I come from the 4th District of Pampanga at dun po ang San Simón Tulaok exit po

**[02:31:23.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003895)  
and the toll plaza ng atin pong NLEX.

**[02:31:29.220] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003896)  
Last week, actually up to this week, from Friday until Tuesday, and today lang po, slight improvement,

**[02:31:36.760] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003897)  
the passengers and commuters on the NLEX northbound and southbound suffered 10 kilometers traffic

**[02:31:45.120] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003898)  
and 10 to 12 hours delay.

**[02:31:48.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003899)  
Mr. Secretary, honorable members, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:31:52.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003900)  
grabe po ang nangyari sa mga tao.

**[02:31:55.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003901)  
People were stuck in their cars for 10 to 12 hours, unable to eat.

**[02:32:00.800] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003902)  
Marami pong nag-CR na lang po along the highway, male and female, I am sorry to let you know, including seniors.

**[02:32:08.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003903)  
And there were a number of very sick people po, yung mga nangangailangan po talaga, who suffered so much.

**[02:32:16.200] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003904)  
As the Chairman of the TRB, could you comment on this and what are your plans?

**[02:32:20.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003905)  
Salamat po, Mr. Chairman, tama po, we commiserate with all the motorists who were stranded during ito po, pagbabaha sa San Simón po natin.

**[02:32:32.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003906)  
Nung nalaman po natin itong pangyayari, hinatasan po natin ng TRB na magbigay ng notice to explain kaagad sa NLEX, para alamin kung ano talaga nangyari.

**[02:32:44.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003907)  
Other than that, there's another memo

**[02:32:48.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003908)  
that was issued also by the TRB.

**[02:32:49.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003909)  
And this is more of, sabihin natin, to study the possibility, to reimburse, kahit pa paano yung manay-stranded sa traffic, kahit pa mas lalo na yung nag-turn back.

**[02:33:02.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003910)  
Ngayon po, may dalawa pong bagay that we have to address.

**[02:33:05.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003911)  
Yung una is the infrastructure itself.

**[02:33:09.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003912)  
Kung anong pong magagawa ng TRB?

**[02:33:11.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003913)  
Ang TRB po kasi is composed of different agencies po.

**[02:33:15.080] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003914)  
Ang nangunguna po dyan is the DOTR, kami po eh for the toll setting.

**[02:33:19.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003915)  
At DPWH po, para tamang infrastructure po doon.

**[02:33:24.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003916)  
Kaapon po, nagpulong na po si Secretary Vince, kasama po ang TRB, kasama din po ang NLEX doon.

**[02:33:30.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003917)  
At mayroong mga plano na po na gagawin, short-term, mid-term, and long-term po.

**[02:33:37.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003918)  
At I was not able to attend the meeting po, andun po si Executive Director JR Tugade.

**[02:33:44.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003919)  
If I may just say po, in a nutshell, yung mga napag-usapan.

**[02:33:49.760] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003920)  
Could you share with us the short, medium, and long-term plans po na pinag-usapan?

**[02:33:55.990] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003921)  
And how soon po can we execute these?

**[02:33:59.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003922)  
Ito po yung napag-usapan din po, at ito yung mga plano po ng DPWH kasama po ang TRB.

**[02:34:06.890] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003923)  
Ang una po yung pagtataas ng Tulaok Pavement by 0.6 meters northbound and 0.5 meters southbound.

**[02:34:15.250] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003924)  
Ito yung mga short-term nila.

**[02:34:17.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003925)  
NLEX Design and Construct Perimeter Floodwall.

**[02:34:21.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003926)  
DPWH po to dredge Tulaok.

**[02:34:24.630] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003927)  
Dredge both trench parallel to NLEX, dito po yung sa gilid.

**[02:34:28.690] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003928)  
Install floodgate and sheet pile to prevent backflow from Tulaok River, both sides of NLEX.

**[02:34:34.710] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003929)  
Deploy heavy-duty siphoning pumps at the trenches to manage the collected water.

**[02:34:39.750] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003930)  
So ito po yung mga sinabmit sa akin ni ED Tugade na maplaplanuhin po ng DPWH kasama po yung NLEX.

**[02:34:48.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003931)  
Ang long-term po nila is to repair collapses.

**[02:34:51.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003932)  
Portion of dike along San Jose at saka dito po sa San Simon.

**[02:34:56.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003933)  
Construction of retarding basin po, impounding detention ponds.

**[02:35:01.510] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003934)  
Naku po ito.

**[02:35:03.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003935)  
Ito po.

**[02:35:04.070] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003936)  
And meron din pong construction of floodway po, from Arayat to Manila.

**[02:35:11.530] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003937)  
So ito po yung overall na plano ng DPWH as part of TRB po.

**[02:35:17.030] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003938)  
Sa panig naman po ng DOTR.

**[02:35:20.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003939)  
Ito po yung...

**[02:35:21.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003940)  
Ang concern namin is more of the motorists.

**[02:35:23.790] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003941)  
So ang inutus po natin sa NLEX, sa mga ganitong pangyayari, inutusan natin ng NLEX na mag-short message na po.

**[02:35:31.630] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003942)  
I-text na po lahat ang mga motorista.

**[02:35:34.510] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003943)

**[02:35:36.750] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003944)  
Kasi meron naman silang numero.

**[02:35:38.650] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003945)  
So we just have to be proactive.

**[02:35:41.250] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003946)  
Hindi po natin sinasabi na kasalanan ng NLEX yung pagbabaha.

**[02:35:45.390] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003947)  
Yesterday po we appealed to them na magbigay kayo ng toll-free.

**[02:35:50.350] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003948)  
And it will be...

**[02:35:51.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003949)

**[02:35:53.530] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003950)

**[02:35:55.230] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003951)

**[02:36:01.030] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003952)  
And they just have to be proactive in informing our motorists na...

**[02:36:06.930] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003953)  
Actually po, yung nakalagay sa sulat, mga three hours before sana, sabihan na.

**[02:36:11.450] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003954)

**[02:36:14.650] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003955)

**[02:36:16.430] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003956)

**[02:36:21.010] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003957)

**[02:36:24.030] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003958)  
Kasi ngayon po merong mga sinasabi ng variable bit-bulitin yata na messaging dun sa NLEX.

**[02:36:31.050] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003959)  
Pero hindi po nakikita kasi.

**[02:36:33.290] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003960)

**[02:36:36.370] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003961)  
May advisory sila sa social media.

**[02:36:38.950] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003962)  
Hindi naman sila nagkulang.

**[02:36:40.810] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003963)  
Pero sa tingin namin, hindi nakakarating sa ating mga motorista.

**[02:36:45.730] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003964)  
At ganun din po, nakikiusap na rin po kami sa aming motorista.

**[02:36:49.390] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003965)  
Kasi alam na nila po.

**[02:36:50.850] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003966)

**[02:36:55.450] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003967)

**[02:37:03.190] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003968)

**[02:37:43.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003976)

**[02:37:58.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003980)

It has to be a holistic program.

**[02:38:12.500] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003983)

**[02:38:23.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003984)

**[02:38:36.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003986)

**[02:38:39.380] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003987)

**[02:38:45.240] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003988)

**[02:38:48.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003989)  
Kasi kung ilang araw po na hindi po makadaan talaga sa NLEX, magkakaroon po tayo.

**[02:39:02.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003992)

**[02:39:11.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-003994)

**[02:39:24.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-003999)

**[02:39:44.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004004)  
Sir, I just wanted to bring up also, I know this is not the Department of Public Works and Highways.

**[02:39:50.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004006)  
We have five...

**[02:40:05.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004010)

**[02:40:21.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004015)

**[02:40:38.080] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004018)

**[02:40:46.960] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004020)

**[02:40:51.540] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004021)  
Ngayon po.

**[02:40:51.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004022)

**[02:40:53.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004023)

**[02:40:55.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004024)

**[02:41:16.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004031)

**[02:42:16.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004050)

**[02:42:18.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004050-split-2)

**[02:42:22.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004050-split-3)  
This is an unsolicited project.

**[02:42:24.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004050-split-4)

**[02:42:28.840] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004051)

**[02:42:30.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004052)

**[02:42:46.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004053)

**[02:42:52.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004054)

**[02:42:58.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004055)  
This was a past administration po. Hindi siya nasama sa mga priority project of the government at that time.

**[02:43:06.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004056)  
At bago po kasi 'yan, ito po'y dumadaan sa maraming sangay ng gobyerno at that time, NEDA and everything.

**[02:43:14.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004057)  
Ang sinasabi po natin ngayon is...

**[02:43:16.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004058)  
Ang alam po namin, ato naman ay the legal process, before a construction should begin or the implementation of a project, may mga kailangan pong dokumento, requisitos, mga sa galing sa mga iba't ibang kagawaran.

**[02:43:33.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004059)

**[02:43:39.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004060)

**[02:43:45.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004061)

**[02:44:01.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004062)

**[02:44:13.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004063)

**[02:44:15.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004064)

**[02:51:35.040] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-004224)  
Salamat po.

**[02:51:35.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004225)

**[02:51:38.660] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004226)

**[02:51:39.600] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004227)  
Cong Anna York.

**[02:51:41.400] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-004228)  
Next to interpolate is Representative Chan.

**[02:51:49.700] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004229)  
Magandang hapon po, Mr. Chair, and to the Department of Transportation.

**[02:51:54.660] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004230)  
So, at the outset, I would just like to express my full support

**[02:51:57.800] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004231)  
for the budget of the Department of Transportation.

**[02:52:00.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004232)  
Meron lang po akong two questions, Secretary.

**[02:52:03.500] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004233)  
So, the first one po is with regard to the published analysis of the Move As One Coalition

**[02:52:10.140] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004234)  
for the fiscal year 2027 National Expenditure Program po,

**[02:52:16.060] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004235)  
na nilabas po noong August 18,

**[02:52:18.240] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004236)  
kung saan na-flag po yun sa Public Transport Modernization Program na

**[02:52:22.200] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004237)  
which fell from 2.5 billion to 176.8 million.

**[02:52:27.560] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004238)  
And the service contracting,

**[02:52:30.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004239)  
from 1 billion to 0,

**[02:52:32.000] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004240)  
fuel subsidies daw from 2.5 billion to 0,

**[02:52:34.980] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004241)  
and the EDSA busway line from 88.7 million to 0.

**[02:52:40.360] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004242)  
So, meron din po kasing letter din po ang Department of Transportation

**[02:52:45.100] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004243)  
dated July 23, I think it was reported po before,

**[02:52:49.920] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004244)  
which was addressed to the Land Bank and the DBP,

**[02:52:53.500] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004245)  
kung saan nag-request po kayo, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:52:56.000] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004246)  
ng moratorium on the Cooperative's Modernization Loans.

**[02:53:00.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004247)  
So, yung pangatlong point lang po is yung August reports

**[02:53:04.980] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004248)  
that modernized jeepney operators have begun receiving foreclosure letters po

**[02:53:10.060] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004249)  
with the LTFRB now negotiating with the banks

**[02:53:13.560] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004250)  
and the Department setting aside 1.5 billion for the sector.

**[02:53:17.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004251)  
So, yung question ko lang naman po, Mr. Secretary, is

**[02:53:19.780] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004252)

**[02:53:25.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004253)  
And will the Department plan or intend,

**[02:53:30.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004254)  
to restore the fuel subsidy and the service contracting lines in the proposal po?

**[02:53:35.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004255)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:53:37.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004256)  
Yes po.

**[02:53:39.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004257)  
Yung una po, yung sagutin ko po yung huling tanong,

**[02:53:41.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004258)  
if you plan to restore po,

**[02:53:43.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004259)  
ang sagot ko po dyan, ipaglalaban po namin through your help po.

**[02:53:46.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004260)  
Makikiusap kami na kailangang maibalik yung budget po,

**[02:53:50.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004261)  
yung pondo po dito sa mga programa.

**[02:53:52.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004262)  
At yung pangalawa po, yung 1.5 billion po na tulong ibibigay natin

**[02:53:57.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004263)

**[02:54:00.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004264)  
Amortization po ng mga yumakap sa programa natin sa modernization,

**[02:54:04.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004265)  
ay yan po naman ay secured in the sense po na

**[02:54:06.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004266)  
ang alam ko po, nagbigay ng DBF.

**[02:54:08.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004267)  
Kanina po nasabi ng NCA,

**[02:54:11.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004268)  
ng 1 billion po, nabigyan na po tayo,

**[02:54:14.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004269)  
ibibigay na natin sa LTFRB po yan.

**[02:54:17.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004270)  
Ganun pa man po, kailangan namin ng karagdagang budget for the 2027.

**[02:54:22.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004271)  
And of course, with your help po, sir,

**[02:54:24.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004272)  
sana po mapondohan natin itong service contracting,

**[02:54:28.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004273)  
itong PTMP,

**[02:54:29.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004274)  
at kung may dadagdag natin yung active busway,

**[02:54:32.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004275)  
yung fuel subsidy.

**[02:54:34.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004276)  
Kami po ay hihingi po ng inyong tulong.

**[02:54:37.760] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004277)  
Apo, Mr. Secretary. Maraming salamat po.

**[02:54:40.240] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004278)  
So, of course, we would like to support that endeavor,

**[02:54:43.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004279)  
lalo na marami pong magbe-benefit po sa ating mga kababayan,

**[02:54:45.920] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004280)  
lalo na po yung mga motorista.

**[02:54:47.780] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004281)  
So, yung pangalawang point ko lang po, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:54:50.480] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004282)  
is really more of an interagency na matter.

**[02:54:54.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004283)  
So, yesterday, I asked the Department of Energy

**[02:54:57.300] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004284)  
about the offshore wind,

**[02:54:59.160] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004285)

**[02:55:00.300] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004286)  
and how the government intends to approach their objective

**[02:55:06.200] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004287)  
of having 21% of the Philippines' energy supply

**[02:55:10.840] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004288)  
come from offshore wind.

**[02:55:13.940] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004289)  
Pero, a big part of that po, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:55:16.660] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004290)  
and I'm sure you'll agree,

**[02:55:17.680] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004291)

**[02:55:21.820] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004292)  
and effort po when it comes to the ports.

**[02:55:24.540] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004293)  
So, to ensure that the ports po sa Philippines

**[02:55:27.840] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004294)  
are ready to accommodate po yung pagdating po

**[02:55:30.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004295)  
ng mga equipment ng para sa offshore wind.

**[02:55:33.120] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004296)  
So, yung gusto ko lang po malaman,

**[02:55:35.060] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004297)  
since natanong ko rin po si Secretary Garin yesterday,

**[02:55:39.460] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004298)  
what will the department po direct the ports authority

**[02:55:43.040] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004299)  
to program for the Jose Panganiban and the Mercedes po?

**[02:55:47.600] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004300)  
And is there a timeline so that the ports are ready

**[02:55:50.880] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004301)  
before the auctions of the green energy auction po

**[02:55:56.520] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004302)  
for the offshore wind?

**[02:55:57.840] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004303)  
We'll need them.

**[02:55:59.420] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004304)  
The winners for that auction will need them.

**[02:56:02.020] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004305)  
And, matanong ko na lang din po yung

**[02:56:04.760] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004306)  
what does 2027 hold for Matnog Port po

**[02:56:07.800] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004307)  
for the Roro Lifeline that carries the traffic

**[02:56:10.320] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004308)  
of the two island groups.

**[02:56:12.340] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004309)  
Marami salamat po, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:56:13.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004310)  
Mr. Chair, before I defer to GMJ po ng PPA po,

**[02:56:18.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004311)  
tama po kayo doon sa offshore wind, no?

**[02:56:20.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004312)  
Yung suporta po diyan ng DOTr,

**[02:56:22.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004313)  
ariyan na pa yung napagusapan namin

**[02:56:23.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004314)  
since last year po with PPA.

**[02:56:25.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004315)  
And, definitely po, PPA will,

**[02:56:27.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004316)  
give details later.

**[02:56:29.480] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004317)  
Yung pangalawa po yung Matnog Port,

**[02:56:31.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004318)  
binisita din po namin yan nung nakaraang taon

**[02:56:35.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004319)  
at may plano rin po ang PPA na magtayo

**[02:56:37.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004320)  
ng panibagong terminal din po dyan sa Matnog Port.

**[02:56:41.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004321)  
Again, Mr. Chairman, may I defer to GMJ po

**[02:56:43.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004322)  
for the details of the project.

**[02:56:46.140] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004323)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:56:47.540] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004324)  
Again, with the permission of our Secretary.

**[02:56:50.100] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004325)  
To update lang po si Pong Chan

**[02:56:53.040] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004326)  
on the two items that he mentioned,

**[02:56:56.080] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004327)  
which is the...

**[02:56:57.840] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004328)  
doon po sa offshore wind port projects natin,

**[02:57:00.260] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004329)  
ang offshore wind port projects po natin,

**[02:57:02.260] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004330)  
ang priority po natin,

**[02:57:03.460] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004331)  
and which is programmed for this year

**[02:57:05.360] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004332)

**[02:57:08.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004333)  
which they've identified na yung priority nila,

**[02:57:11.200] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004334)
