**[02:57:14.980] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004335)  
So, the contract for the...

**[02:57:18.280] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004336)  
for that offshore wind terminal,

**[02:57:21.120] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004337)  
your honors,

**[02:57:22.240] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004338)  
has already been awarded,

**[02:57:23.760] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004339)  
has been bidded out,

**[02:57:25.160] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004340)  
and has been awarded.

**[02:57:26.480] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004341)  
In fact, we are proud to say that,

**[02:57:27.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004342)  
yun pong scheme po nung terminal na yun

**[02:57:33.480] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004343)  
is on a turnkey basis.

**[02:57:35.460] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004344)  
So, there is no mobilization,

**[02:57:38.760] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004345)  
there is no down payment,

**[02:57:40.120] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004346)  
there is no advance payment,

**[02:57:41.880] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004347)  
and the government will only pay

**[02:57:43.980] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004348)  
after turnover within a period of three years.

**[02:57:49.200] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004349)  
Yan po.

**[02:57:50.040] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004350)  
And then, that will commence construction po

**[02:57:52.960] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004351)  
within, I think, by October.

**[02:57:55.680] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004352)  
Mag-breakground.

**[02:57:57.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004353)  
Kung na po tayo doon.

**[02:57:59.220] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004354)  
And in coordination po with the schedule

**[02:58:01.180] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004355)  
and timeline of the Department of Energy,

**[02:58:03.920] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004356)  
we anticipate that it will be usable

**[02:58:06.380] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004357)  
by the service contractors

**[02:58:08.960] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004358)  
of the Department of Energy

**[02:58:10.640] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004359)  
to enable them to produce the first kilowatt

**[02:58:15.760] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004360)  
by 2028,

**[02:58:17.940] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004361)  
by the first half of 2028.

**[02:58:20.740] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004362)  
Doon naman po sa Matnog Port po natin,

**[02:58:24.220] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004363)  
we will be bidding out,

**[02:58:27.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004364)  
the next phase of the expansion of Matnog Port

**[02:58:31.180] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004365)  
before the end of the year po,

**[02:58:33.540] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004366)  
and we will commence construction there.

**[02:58:35.240] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004367)  
Ang second phase po natin doon

**[02:58:36.860] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004368)  
includes the further expansion of Matnog Port

**[02:58:39.960] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004369)  
together with the new passenger terminal building

**[02:58:43.000] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004370)  
in Matnog.

**[02:58:44.420] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004371)  
And then, the subsequent phase po dyan,

**[02:58:46.820] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004372)  
which is phase three,

**[02:58:48.240] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004373)  
which we will program for next year,

**[02:58:50.620] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004374)  
and hopefully by next year,

**[02:58:52.120] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004375)  
we can commence phase three

**[02:58:53.900] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004376)  
just in time po na matatapos yung phase two natin.

**[02:58:57.060] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004377)  
Okay.

**[02:58:57.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004378)  
So that will now be the final expansion po,

**[02:59:00.860] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004379)  
Matnog,

**[02:59:01.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004380)  
if you are familiar po,

**[02:59:03.140] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004381)  
Kong, your honor,

**[02:59:04.680] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004382)  
kung saan po ngayon yung Matnog Terminal natin,

**[02:59:08.180] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004383)  
we will extend that for us full development

**[02:59:10.620] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004384)  
all the way doon po sa may coastal road,

**[02:59:14.200] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004385)  
kukunin na po natin yung buong yun

**[02:59:16.120] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004386)  
for the expansion so that

**[02:59:17.540] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004387)  
we will no longer experience na yung mga

**[02:59:21.020] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004388)  
biyahero natin

**[02:59:22.640] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004389)

**[02:59:24.720] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004390)

**[02:59:27.800] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004391)  
Doon na po sila paparado sa loob po ng terminal.

**[02:59:30.220] PPA General Manager Jay Daniel R. Santiago** (asr-004392)  
Yun na po, your honors.

**[02:59:34.540] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004393)  
Actually, yun po, maraming salamat po.

**[02:59:36.540] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004394)

**[02:59:38.400] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004395)

**[02:59:41.220] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004396)

**[02:59:43.440] Rep. Jan Franz Norbert Joselito A. Chan** (asr-004397)

that the Department of Transportation also has

So, maraming salamat po.

**[03:00:14.010] Rep. Henry "Indy" F. Oaminal Jr.** (asr-004407)

**[03:00:23.890] Rep. Henry "Indy" F. Oaminal Jr.** (asr-004408)

Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[03:00:49.960] Rep. Henry "Indy" F. Oaminal Jr.** (asr-004410)

**[03:03:36.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004443)

**[03:03:42.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004444)

**[03:03:47.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004445)

**[03:04:10.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004449)

**[03:04:15.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004450)

**[03:27:50.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004691)

**[03:27:56.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004693)

**[03:27:58.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004694)

**[03:28:04.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004695)

**[03:28:07.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004696)  
Ang nangyayari po minsan, meron pong request ang ating mga lokal na pamahalaan na gusto rin naman nilang mag-implement ng ganitong active transport.

**[03:28:18.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004697)

**[03:28:25.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004698)  
na para mas mapabilis ang pag-i-implement ng proyekto at makikipagtulungan kami sa mga LGU po,

**[03:28:33.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004699)  
eh aayusin po namin ang aming polisiya.

**[03:28:36.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004700)  
Pero hindi po namin makakalimutan na nangyayari lang po ito, napondo dahil pinunduhan po ng mga kinatawan ng mga distrito.

**[03:28:43.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004701)  
So kung iyan ay nagiging direksyon, maaari din bang i-download iyan sa kanila?

**[03:28:51.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004702)  
Yes.

**[03:28:52.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004703)  
Ilang beses na po na kay

**[03:28:53.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004704)  
To Secretary Bautista?

**[03:28:54.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004705)  
Secretary Bautista humingi po ng dispensa.

**[03:28:57.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004706)

**[03:28:59.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004707)  
Nawala si Secretary Bautista.

**[03:29:01.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004708)  
Nag-request ako kay Secretary Vince.

**[03:29:04.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004709)  
Sumulat ako patungkol

**[03:29:05.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004710)  
uli sa parehong bagay.

**[03:29:09.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004711)  
Sumulat ako sa kanya nung nakaraang taon, April at July of 2025.

**[03:29:14.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004712)  
Usap kami.

**[03:29:15.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004713)  
Okay, no problem.

**[03:29:16.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004714)  
Nawala siya.

**[03:29:17.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004715)  
Kayo po ang pumalit.

**[03:29:19.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004716)  
Nagulat na lang po ako that that same request, in-approve, was now being

**[03:29:22.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004717)  
implemented.

**[03:29:23.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004718)  
Nababalitaan ko na lang afterwards.

**[03:29:25.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004719)  
So sabi ko sa mga kasama ko, papaano ito?

**[03:29:29.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004720)  
Tayo nag-a-approve ng budget.

**[03:29:31.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004721)  
How are we able to perform our oversight functions when we don't even get invited to the occasion

**[03:29:38.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004722)

**[03:29:43.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004723)  
Nalalaman na lang po namin sa iba.

**[03:29:46.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004724)  
So mahirap po kasi, siyempre it's also part of our function, you have to understand,

**[03:29:51.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004725)

**[03:29:54.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004726)

**[03:29:58.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004727)  
Pero paano namin mapupuri

**[03:30:00.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004728)

**[03:30:04.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004729)  
At tama po Mr. Chairman, iyan po ay pagkakamali sa aking panig.

**[03:30:09.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004730)  
Pagdating po kasi sa mga inauguration, sa mga groundbreaking,

**[03:30:14.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004731)

**[03:30:16.880] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004732)  
Pero mula po nung tama po kayo, nung tinawagan nyo po ako,

**[03:30:20.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004733)  
at sinabi nyo nga po na may ganito mga pangyayari,

**[03:30:23.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004734)  
ako po ay humingi ka agad ng paumanhin.

**[03:30:26.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004735)  
At ang gagawin ko nga po ngayon, nakaugalihan ko na rin po,

**[03:30:29.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004736)

**[03:30:32.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004737)  
My general instruction is always to invite everyone po.

**[03:30:37.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004738)  
At ang tingin kasi namin ay isang proyekto ay para sa isang komunidad.

**[03:30:42.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004739)  
At sana lahat po ng mga leader at pinuno sa lugar na yan ay maimbitahan po.

**[03:30:50.300] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004740)  
Hindi lang, ang imbitasyon ay isang mababaw na bagay.

**[03:30:53.640] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004741)  
Pero ibig sabihin nyo, active participation.

**[03:30:55.560] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004742)  
So that we know that the funds for which we are being supported,

**[03:30:59.300] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004743)  
sought, for which kami nanguhusgan, ay nagagamit ng tama.

**[03:31:03.120] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004744)  
Yan lang naman po ang, hindi naman yung magpakita ka daw, magpapicture.

**[03:31:06.860] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004745)  
That's not really the most relevant thing.

**[03:31:08.860] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004746)  
It is the proper use of funds entrusted to us in the pursuit of our,

**[03:31:15.600] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004747)  
as the, having the power of the purse, Secretary.

**[03:31:20.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004748)  
I fully agree po.

**[03:31:22.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004749)  
Kaya nga po makikiusap kami na ngayon po sa special provision po natin nangga.

**[03:31:26.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004750)  
Sana po mabigyan nyo pa na kami ng mas malawak.

**[03:31:29.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004751)  
Na pabaga opportunity so that we can download it to the proper barangay.

**[03:31:36.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004752)  
At sa ganun din po we can report po dun sa mga congressman na talaga nagbigay ng pondo

**[03:31:42.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004753)

**[03:31:46.940] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004754)  
My time is up.

**[03:31:49.580] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004755)  
Ayoko na po masyadong mag-consume ng time.

**[03:31:53.660] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004756)  
Pero sana lang is kapag dinadownload natin ito,

**[03:31:57.420] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004757)  
sa kahit anumang,

**[03:31:59.160] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004763)

**[03:51:49.820] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-005281)  
Chairman, please wrap up your question.

**[03:51:52.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005282)  
May I just answer, Mr. Chair?

**[03:51:54.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005283)  
Kaya nga po, parati kong sinasabi kanina, kami nakikiusap, nabigyan kami ng pondo.

**[03:51:59.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005284)  
At ang parati ko pong posisyon, mas kailangan natin ngayon na paigtingin yung ating modernization program.

**[03:52:07.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005285)  
Nang kulang po ang ating pondo na nabigay sa NEP.

**[03:52:09.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005286)  
Just like last year po, ang NEP po natin, I think, sa PTMP, ay mga 1.2, pero tinaasan nyo po, plus 300 million.

**[03:52:19.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005287)  
Yung 300 million po, yan, is subject to SARO po ngayon.

**[03:52:22.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005288)  
Kaya 1.2 pa lang yata yung nabigyan o 1 billion.

**[03:52:24.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005289)  
Thereabout po.

**[03:52:25.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005290)  
Ngayon, ulitin ko po, matutuloy lang po yung modernization program.

**[03:52:30.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005291)  
Gaganda po yan kung tayo po ay may sapat na pondo.

**[03:52:34.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005292)  
At kaya nga po, kami nakikiusap sa Kongreso na sana po, mabigyan kami ng sapat na pondo para po mas mapalawak natin yung modernization program natin.

**[03:52:43.220] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005293)  
Mr. Chair, for your intelligence, kwant lang ha? Puti na lang, no?

**[03:52:46.780] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005294)  
Kaya nga, ako seryoso dito na magtanong dahil nakita ko yung trend, eh.

**[03:52:52.620] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005295)  
Yung modernization natin ngayon, patuloy yung extension, walang hinto ang consolidation.

**[03:52:59.980] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005296)  
Sabihin, parang wala tayong klaro na direction kung paano natin tuldukan itong, kasi diba, part of the program is consolidation.

**[03:53:09.780] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005297)  
Pag hindi mag-consolidate, diba, wala.

**[03:53:12.860] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005298)  
Kasi yun na ang direction ng ating program.

**[03:53:15.200] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005299)  
Now, here come, nakita natin out from the budget, parang makikita mo yung trend.

**[03:53:19.420] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005300)  
How serious are we with this program?

**[03:53:21.500] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005301)  
So, tingnan mo, what are you going to do with the cooperatives na nag-invest na sa modernization, tapos yung iba hindi na magtuloy-tuloy?

**[03:53:28.680] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005302)  
Kasi, inayagan man natin.

**[03:53:29.960] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005303)  
Kasi, inayagan man natin yung mga hindi nag-consolidate na mag-renew rin nyo.

**[03:53:33.860] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005304)  
Nakailang MC na tayo to renew the franchise?

**[03:53:37.180] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005305)  
Diba, three months, six months, hanggang ngayon, na-extend, na-extend.

**[03:53:41.720] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005306)  
Sabi ba daw, narinig ko ngayon, hanggang July 31 na nag-end na tayo.

**[03:53:48.200] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005307)  
So, ang tanong dito, ilan ba ang nag-consolidate?

**[03:53:51.740] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005308)  
Dahil binigyan natin sila since 2024 until July 31, 2026, ng extension.

**[03:53:58.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005309)  
Kasi, yung ibang transport operator, pag ayaw talaga mag-consolidate yun, kahit anong extend mo dun, hindi yan magko-consolidate.

**[03:54:05.760] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005310)  
That's my proposition here.

**[03:54:09.260] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005311)  
So, that's why, ang tanong ko dito, how serious are we na ituloy itong PTMP para mag-guide din kami dito sa house?

**[03:54:18.920] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005312)  
Kasi, we will support this kung yung direksyon nyo is really to look for this program.

**[03:54:26.400] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005313)  
Kasi, una-una, maganda naman.

**[03:54:28.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005314)  
Ito, niyakap ito ng mga transport group natin, nag-operativize sila, nag-corporation sila, but then, what happened right now?

**[03:54:37.960] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005315)  
Maraming na foreclose, anong rason dun?

**[03:54:40.420] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005316)  
Dahil, walang tigil ang pagtakbo ng ibang entity na hindi nag-consolidate kasi pwede naman pala sila magbiyahe na hindi rin pila sila nag-consolidated.

**[03:54:52.260] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005317)  
So, again, wala tayong pundo for this program.

**[03:54:56.040] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005318)  
Damitin natin na rason yan.

**[03:54:58.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005319)  
Cannot continue anymore because we have no fund.

**[03:55:01.100] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005320)  
Okay?

**[03:55:01.640] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005321)  
So, that's why, Mr. Chair, with this committee, we will get the commitment of our agency kung seryoso sa katuloy natin itong program.

**[03:55:09.640] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005322)  
Kung hindi, sabihan nyo kami na bayaran na lang yung nag-modernize na nag-utang para magkanya-kanya na at ibang programa ang gagawin natin.

**[03:55:21.760] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005323)  
But then, if you are serious, I have a bill pending here.

**[03:55:26.280] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005324)  
Na i-institutionalize sana itong PTMP.

**[03:55:28.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005325)  
Pero kung hindi kayo seryoso dito, what's the use of this bill na ma-institutionalize itong ating PTMP?

**[03:55:35.200] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005326)  
Okay?

**[03:55:35.440] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005327)  
So, that's why, related dito, Mr. Chair, sa budget, na pag wala kayong budget, hindi nyo tumatuloy.

**[03:55:41.640] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005328)  
So, again, we will be getting you because the next manifestation I could have, of course, to provide with this committee na ibalik natin yung budget ng PTMP para magtuloy-tuloy tayo.

**[03:55:54.740] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005329)  
Pero pag sinabi nyo dito na wala na kayo magawa, hindi nyo tumatuloy.

**[03:55:58.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005330)  
Kung hindi nyo na kaya, hindi sabihan nyo kami ng totoo.

**[03:56:02.080] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005331)  
At ang tanong dito is, alam ba ng Presidente?

**[03:56:05.260] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005332)  
Pag sinabi natin na ayaw na nyo ituloy.

**[03:56:07.920] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005333)  
So, yun lang, Mr. Chair, I will be getting the commitment of the DUTR family on this program kasi malaking dagok to sa transport sector natin.

**[03:56:17.500] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005334)  
Especially itong mga co-ops na nirepresentahan ko na nag-invest, niyakap yung program, gumastos, tapos ngayon, sumakit ulo nila.

**[03:56:26.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005335)  
Ang laki na ng utang, napu-foreclose na yung kanilang mga unit, natitingga na dahil wala kayong ginawang tamang proseso dito.

**[03:56:35.420] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005336)  
May LTPRP pa tayo na issue.

**[03:56:38.320] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005337)  
Yung malaki din sa gabal na kung tingnan natin yung figure right now, parang walang nangyari, walang improvement.

**[03:56:45.260] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005338)  
So, walang budget.

**[03:56:47.480] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005339)  
So, how are you going to continue?

**[03:56:48.960] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005340)  
So, again, Mr. Chair, ibalik ko.

**[03:56:51.840] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005341)  
Can I get the position of the DUTR on this program?

**[03:56:56.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005342)  
Yun ba talaga-talaga ang totoong mandato na with your position right now, Sec, ituloy o hindi?

**[03:57:04.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005343)  
Opo, itutuloy po natin yung PTMP. Napakagandang konsepto po ng PTMP.

**[03:57:10.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005344)  
Pero admittedly po, ang problema is the implementation. Yun po yung kailangan naming ayusin.

**[03:57:15.260] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005345)  
Kaya nga, dahil yun nga, ulong-sulong kasi tayo sa gusto natin mangyari.

**[03:57:21.280] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005346)  
Imagine mo, 86% na nag-take rest, niyakap yung program, tapos...

**[03:57:26.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005347)  
Pa-extend na-extend tayo just to consider yung mga ilan na lang din.

**[03:57:31.800] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005348)  
So, pag ganyan tayo, tingnan mo yung nangyari sa nag-consolidate ngayon,

**[03:57:36.000] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005349)  
ayaw na nila ibigay yung kanilang PA para for the CPC or for the consolidation.

**[03:57:41.380] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005350)  
Dahil alam nila, i-can operate naman na magkanya-kanya pa rin.

**[03:57:44.620] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005351)  
So, kung hindi nyo yan maayos, walang kwenta itong ating PTMP at we cannot move forward.

**[03:57:53.160] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005352)  
Okay, so, again, Mr. Chair, alam ko na kailangan ang equity.

**[03:57:56.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005353)  
Ang ating fuel subsidy, dahil tumaas ang fuel ngayon, tapos yung service contracting na part of the component of the program,

**[03:58:08.040] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005354)  
dapat may balik out from their first request.

**[03:58:12.480] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005355)  
Alam ko, nag-request kayo, hindi napagbigyan.

**[03:58:15.040] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005356)  
So, question there is that, kung hindi tayo seryoso dito, we cannot do otherwise sa part ng House of Representatives,

**[03:58:23.120] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005357)  
na kung saan we could further...

**[03:58:26.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005358)  
provide more budget on this.

**[03:58:28.700] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005359)  
So, salamat for the commitment, at least narinig na ang ating transport group na very serious tayo.

**[03:58:35.380] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005360)  
At kami naman dito sa House of Representatives, we are supportive for the good ng ating commuters dito.

**[03:58:42.580] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005361)  
At maprotektahan natin yung nag-invest at yung sector na kung saan sila nag-yakap sa program,

**[03:58:50.780] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005362)  
rather than yung nailan na ayaw talaga mag-consolidate.

**[03:58:53.980] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005363)  
So, haya na natin sila.

**[03:58:55.480] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005364)  
And then,

**[03:58:56.040] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005365)  
let's move forward sa mga gustong mag-continue sa programa para wala na tayong baggages dito.

**[03:59:03.380] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005366)  
Okay?

**[03:59:03.940] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005367)  
So, yun lang, Mr. Chair.

**[03:59:05.100] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005368)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:59:05.820] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005369)  
With my request kanina, kung pwede mabigyan tayo ng mga data na yan, kasi kailangan din namin for processes dito.

**[03:59:13.640] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005370)  
At the same time, I am a manifestation, Mr. Chair, of support yung request ng DUTR,

**[03:59:18.720] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005371)  
kung pwede i-reconstitute yung kanilang natanggal na request for the PT&P program.

**[03:59:24.800] Rep. Felimon M. Espares** (asr-005372)  
Thank you very much.

**[03:59:26.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-005373)  
Thank you, Chairman Mon Espares.

**[03:59:28.040] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-005374)  
And we request everyone to please settle down, where we still have an ongoing committee hearing.

**[03:59:34.820] Rep. Edwin Ongchuan** (asr-005375)  
Thank you.

Mr. Chair, if I may,

**[04:01:43.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005431)  
tama po kayo,

We're going to request the same

sa ADB na hindi po mahinto

lang pagdating sa mga kalsada at tulay

at isama na rin po yung

At inatasan na rin

sa USEC-TJ, nasulatan na rin po

**[04:16:40.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005816)

**[04:16:49.400] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005817)  
Mr. Chair, yung pong latest po na incident na nagkaroon po ng tubig sa loob po ng train,

**[04:16:55.820] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005818)  
hindi po ito galing.

**[04:16:57.500] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005819)  
Sa labas, hindi rin po ito problema sa roofing ng train.

**[04:17:00.500] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005820)  
Ang nangyayari lang po is that,

**[04:17:02.480] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005821)  
nagka-problema po yung drainage nung kanilang aircon ng train.

**[04:17:06.680] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005822)  
Kaya po yung tubig na nanggagaling po doon sa aircon, naipon po doon sa loob ng train.

**[04:17:12.460] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005823)  
At eventually, nung hindi na po gumagana yung drainage,

**[04:17:16.080] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005824)  
tumapon po sa loob ng train yung tubig na nanggagaling po sa aircon.

**[04:17:20.900] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005825)  
So, ang punot-dulo po is maintenance po.

**[04:17:25.720] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005826)  
Kailangan lang pong linisin nila.

**[04:17:27.500] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005827)  
Yung drainage or palitan po yung mga sirang parts doon sa drainage system po nung train.

**[04:17:32.080] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005828)  
At sinitan po namin ang LRMC.

**[04:17:35.040] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005829)  
Actually, ito pong nakaraang incident, parang pangatlo na po yan.

**[04:17:39.240] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005830)  
At yung mga dalawang na unang incident, sinitan na po namin sila formally.

**[04:17:43.280] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005831)  
Nakatatlong sulat na po kami sa kanila.

**[04:17:45.600] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005832)  
And doon po sa ibang mga bagay, like sa condition po ng mga CR nila, yung cleanliness po ng estasyon,

**[04:17:52.340] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005833)  
marami po tayong mga staff na nandoon, mga empleyado po ng LRT.

**[04:17:55.920] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005834)  
At binabantayan po namin sila.

**[04:17:57.500] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005835)  
Actually, everyday po, nagbibigay kami ng citation sa mga violations po nila.

**[04:18:02.220] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005836)  
Like maruming CR, maruming flooring. So lahat po.

**[04:18:05.640] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005837)  
In fact, under the concession agreement po, may penalty po sila.

**[04:18:09.300] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005838)  
So far, sa ngayon, ang kanilang penalty na sinisingil namin is umabot na po ng 121 million.

**[04:18:16.780] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005839)  
Yun po yung outstanding na penalty po nila.

**[04:18:19.540] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005840)  
Pero since meron po kami usapin tungkol sa mga utang naman natin sa kanila, nakapinbin po ito.

**[04:18:24.880] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005841)  
Pero eventually po, isisingil po namin din.

**[04:18:27.500] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005842)  
Itong amount na ito, galing po sa kanila.

**[04:18:30.800] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005843)  
Kailangan po nilang bayaran or i-offset nila sa mga sinisingil po nila sa gobyerno.

**[04:18:35.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005844)  
Okay.

**[04:18:36.420] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005845)  
Yun din po yung gusto nating maipunto na kung may accountability po dito, it would be LRMC.

**[04:18:46.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005846)  
Meron pong concession agreement and then merong pag-insure na yung quality standards ay nasusunod.

**[04:18:53.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005847)  
And so, natuwa ako na marinig na may ganong formal.

**[04:18:57.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005848)  
Napag-sita.

**[04:18:58.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005849)  
At mangingi po kami ng update, written update ng mga naging steps so far para po essentially i-hold accountable yung LRMC doon sa mga violations sa basic concession agreement.

**[04:19:10.820] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005850)  
Pero sa atin, hindi po dapat yung low-level staff yung iberate.

**[04:19:16.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005851)  
It is dapat formally through LRMC.

**[04:19:18.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005852)  
So, mangingi po tayo ng kopya ng ganun kung maaari po, Mr. Chair.

**[04:19:21.840] LRTA Administrator Atty. Hernando T. Cabrera** (asr-005853)  
Opo, ibibigay po namin po lahat.

**[04:19:24.680] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005854)  
Siguro ang last questions ko na lang po ay it would be,

**[04:19:27.500] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005855)  
in relation to NAIA, dahil po yun nga po in the past and in the recent years ay ito po ay binenta po sa NNIC.

**[04:19:39.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005856)  
May question po tayo na, wait lang po Chair, pause lang po, saglit lang.

**[04:19:57.880] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005857)  
Pwede po bang malaman kung, Mr. Chair, magkano yung total na nakolekta ng NNIC sa mga passengers ng NAIA?

**[04:20:06.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005858)  
Is this?

**[04:20:07.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005859)  
Information that can be submitted?

**[04:20:14.710] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005860)  
I think we're referring to passenger service charge po.

**[04:20:19.210] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005861)  
Is that it po?

**[04:20:20.170] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005862)  
The submission or the remittance of NNIC to the government.

**[04:20:23.950] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005863)  
Kasi so far po, the NNIC remitted to the government around 78 billion pesos.

**[04:20:33.410] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005864)  
Aside from the remittance po, andyan din po yung basically total na nakolek nila.

**[04:20:38.850] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005865)  
Hindi lang din po sa passenger service charge.

**[04:20:40.570] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005866)  
Pero lahat po nang nakolek nilang income ever since taking over NAIA.

**[04:20:46.350] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005867)  
It would include sana din po revenues sa lahat ng mga businesses and private operators sa NAIA.

**[04:20:52.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005868)  
At ang mga nakita sa mga other operations sa NAIA, including din siyempre, other than concessionaires, yung mga aircraft carriers.

**[04:21:02.610] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005869)  
Can that be submitted, Mr. Chair?

**[04:21:04.990] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005870)  
We will comply, Mr. Chair.

**[04:21:07.030] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005871)  
Mr. Chair, I ask this question dahil,

**[04:21:10.570] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005872)  
narinig na natin in other instances, yung magkano nang na-remit ng NNIC sa gobyerno.

**[04:21:16.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005873)  
Pero hindi din po natin nakita yung other side of the picture, which is magkano na po ang income na nakuha nila since the purchase of NAIA through the passengers.

**[04:21:26.110] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005874)  
Given nga po na nagkaroon ng passenger service charge increase, kailangan din natin po i-assess.

**[04:21:31.950] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005875)  
Doon sa kung magkano yung na-remit and even kung magkano pa yung natitira na para matapos yung agreement.

**[04:21:40.570] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005876)  
Magkano na ang nakuha in income?

**[04:21:44.110] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005877)  
Mr. Chair, we will be submitting informal letter na lang with the committee also furnished dun sa request po natin for data.

**[04:21:52.390] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-005878)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chair.

**[04:21:57.640] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-005879)  
Thank you very much, Cong Renee Co.

**[04:22:00.200] Rep. Reynante U. Arrogancia** (asr-005880)  
Next to interpolate for the second round, and I hope this is the last, Representative Franz Pumaren.

**[04:22:08.180] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005881)  
Yes, thank you very much.

**[04:22:09.100] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005882)  
Actually, dapat hindi na ako magtatanong.

**[04:22:11.020] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005883)  
Pero nandito na rin si GM.

**[04:22:13.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005884)  
Sayang naman ang appearance niya.

**[04:22:15.260] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005885)  
Diba, kung hindi natin tatanungin, I know, kayang-kaya ni Sec. Banoy sagutin to.

**[04:22:21.740] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005886)  
Is this, there's still hope pa ba dun sa Bicol Express natin?

**[04:22:26.200] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005887)  
Because I know yung loan natin with China will be expiring this year.

**[04:22:31.520] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005888)  
Tama po ba?

**[04:22:33.280] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005889)  
With the permission of Sec. Banoy and Mr. Chair.

**[04:22:37.840] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005890)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:22:38.880] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005891)  
There's still a hope.

**[04:22:39.920] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005892)  
Actually, we're continuing yung rehabilitation pa rin.

**[04:22:43.460] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005893)  
Yung line natin, yung dadalawa lang pong tren na nag-ooperate ngayon.

**[04:22:48.780] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005894)  
Dito sa Lucena hanggang Calamba at saka sa Lupi, Camarines Sur hanggang Naga.

**[04:22:54.480] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005895)  
Yung Naga to Legazpi was damaged last year due to the typhoon.

**[04:22:58.120] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005896)  
But we are restoring that with the budget that we have for 2026.

**[04:23:02.340] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005897)  
What we need to complete the 422-kilometer line is the rehabilitation po ng mula Lucena, Quezon.

**[04:23:09.340] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005898)  
Idudugtong 'yun hanggang doon sa Lupi, Camarines Sur.

**[04:23:11.580] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005899)  
And those are the things na sinasama po

**[04:23:13.460] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005900)  
namin sa request ng budget sana.

**[04:23:16.160] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005901)  
Which we hope the Congress, through the support of course of our Department of Transportation,

**[04:23:23.400] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005902)  
endorsing our request for additional budget para po sa capital outlay ng PNR po.

**[04:23:28.600] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005903)  
You know, alam ko yung sinasabi nyo, always there's hope.

**[04:23:31.420] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005904)  
But my main question ko dyan, is it worth the investment?

**[04:23:34.640] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005905)  
When in fact, do you have data kung ilan sumasakay ng papuntang Bicol Express?

**[04:23:40.180] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005906)  
Because expiring na yung loan natin.

**[04:23:43.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005907)  
That's about 6 billion.

**[04:23:44.740] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005908)  
Tapos, I guess you will be requesting Congress for additional funding.

**[04:23:50.800] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005909)  
Do you think yung marirecover pa ba natin yung investment dyan?

**[04:23:54.680] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005910)  
Do we have to look at the whole picture?

**[04:23:57.960] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005911)  
Mr. Chair, ngayon po sa kasalukuyan, honestly, we have only around 1,600 daily ridership.

**[04:24:06.400] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005912)  
But with the migration of the trains na nung mga trains na nandito sa Manila na may migrate noong tumigil ang operasyon,

**[04:24:13.460] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005913)  
sa Manila, at idadagdag sa operasyon, that passenger is expected po na tataas po kami ng mga around 5,000 to 6,000 daily.

**[04:24:23.100] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005914)  
But with the additional trains po para sa Long Haul, I think, especially sa tourist destination pang Bicol,

**[04:24:29.020] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005915)  
connecting the Region 4 to Region 5, that will increase more passenger po.

**[04:24:34.540] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005916)  
At our expectation based doon sa study, initial study ng original alignment for South Long Haul,

**[04:24:39.540] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005917)  
passing through the original PNR alignment,

**[04:24:43.460] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005918)  
approximately around 20,000 to 25,000 daily ridership po ang pwede po mabigyan ng servisyo ng Philippine National Railway pa.

**[04:24:52.940] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005919)  
Okay, ganito lang, GM. You can give me all those data, but we want it in writing.

**[04:24:58.260] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005920)  
So before we bring this to the plenary, if you can submit us all the data, justification, is it worth investing,

**[04:25:07.140] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005921)  
is it worth pushing yung proyektong yan? Okay?

**[04:25:12.180] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005922)  
Mr. Chair, yes.

**[04:25:13.460] PNR General Manager Deovanni S. Miranda** (asr-005923)  
Yes, we will submit po, of course, in coordination to our Secretary Banay po.

**[04:25:18.960] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005924)  
Okay, next, yung paborito ko, Marina. Okay.

**[04:25:24.300] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005925)  
Kamusta yung Orient Register Classification? Suspended na ba yun? Blacklisted na ba yun?

**[04:25:30.340] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005926)  
Mr. Chair, still suspended pa po under the preventive suspension.

**[04:25:35.640] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005927)  
So, alam ko suspended. I'm just trying to ask your opinion. Blacklisted na ba yun?

**[04:25:39.940] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005928)  
Not yet po. So, meron pa pong...

**[04:25:43.460] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005929)  
Still, actually, nasa Office of the President.

**[04:25:46.360] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005930)  
You know, sino may appeal? Sila?

**[04:25:48.300] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005931)  
Sila po.

**[04:25:49.140] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005932)  
But who will give the recommendation? It's Marina, right? Hindi naman alam ng Presidente yan eh.

**[04:25:54.960] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005933)  
So, ang point ko dito, why is this Orient Classification is so special?

**[04:26:00.340] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005934)  
Ilan palpak na to? Ilan na namatay na tao na hindi natin blacklist?

**[04:26:06.360] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005935)  
It doesn't make sense.

**[04:26:09.380] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005936)  
Kaya siguro kahit kayo nag-a-appeal na mag-increase ng pondo,

**[04:26:13.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005937)  
ako mismo nga haharang eh.

**[04:26:17.250] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005938)  
Give me a very good justification for that.

**[04:26:20.350] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005939)  
Ongoing na, sir. Actually, Mr. Chair, yung kanilang MR,

**[04:26:25.630] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005940)  
nag-appel na po ng Marina. So, yung last, ano po, na appeal...

**[04:26:30.930] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005941)  
Ma'am, alam ko na po yan. Laging yan sinasabi nyo na kailang hearing na tayo.

**[04:26:36.590] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005942)  
Ilang buwan na yung may mga namatay.

**[04:26:39.050] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005943)  
Pero that I cannot understand. What's so special about that Classification Society?

**[04:26:44.230] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005944)  
Okay.

**[04:26:45.410] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005945)  
Ang dami ng palpak niya, puro suspension.

**[04:26:49.670] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005946)  
'Yun pag ganyan nga, bina-blacklist na 'yan eh.

**[04:26:52.270] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005947)  
Sa LTO nga, pag may nakasagasa and everything, tinatanggalan na ng lisensya kaagad 'yun eh.

**[04:26:57.410] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005948)  
With this one, there's something special with that.

**[04:27:00.390] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005949)  
With that Classification Society.

**[04:27:03.750] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005950)  
Ilan na yung Classification Society nyo na-accredited?

**[04:27:06.990] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005951)  
We have three po na local, of course, including Orient.

**[04:27:11.670] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005952)  
And we have seven international.

**[04:27:15.410] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005953)  
Members of the IACS.

**[04:27:17.050] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005954)  
Nag-a-admit ba kayo ng mga ibang gustong maging part ng Classification Society?

**[04:27:22.550] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005955)  
So far po, kinontinue ko lang po yung moratorium that was issued, I think, 2020.

**[04:27:30.350] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005956)  
No, no, no. I understand that.

**[04:27:31.770] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005957)  
But you should understand, hindi mo naman kailangan maging genius sa Marina.

**[04:27:35.770] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005958)  
6,000, 7,000 yung mga boats na ganyan.

**[04:27:39.470] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005959)  
Sa sampo lang yung nag-classify.

**[04:27:41.670] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005960)  
How can they accommodate to classify that number of ships?

**[04:27:44.790] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005961)  
Bakit ayaw natin magpatanggap, magpasok ng bagong Classification Society?

**[04:27:49.330] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005962)  
Are we trying to control it?

**[04:27:50.690] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005963)  
Are we favoring certain Classification Society?

**[04:27:53.710] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005964)  
It doesn't make sense.

**[04:27:57.020] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005965)  
Wala naman pong finifavor.

**[04:27:59.240] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005966)  
Actually, yung naunang thrust ng administration ay similar to Japan and Indonesia.

**[04:28:07.580] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005967)  
Merong isang local for the domestic and then we accept po yung mga members of the IACS.

**[04:28:15.200] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005968)  
Okay.

**[04:28:16.200] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005969)  
Tama yung sinasabi mo.

**[04:28:17.660] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005970)  
But you're looking at 6,000, 7,000 ships.

**[04:28:21.380] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005971)  
I couldn't understand how can you really classify them properly without rushing,

**[04:28:27.060] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005972)  
without making a valid classification.

**[04:28:32.820] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005973)  
But anyway, I rest my case with you.

**[04:28:36.600] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005974)  
I leave it up to the Secretary.

**[04:28:39.040] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005975)  
But I still believe kung ano mga nangyayari dyan up to now.

**[04:28:44.960] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005976)  
Anyway, with the current improvement,

**[04:28:46.200] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005977)  
yung transportation sector is going under the leadership of SecPanoy.

**[04:28:52.900] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005978)  
We can be assured that the only roadworthy vehicles and seaworthy vessels will serve the public.

**[04:28:59.180] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005979)  
However, we must remember that accidents can still happen.

**[04:29:03.120] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005980)  
Hence, before LTFRB or Marina issues any authority, regular or provisional,

**[04:29:09.140] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005981)  
the riding public should always be secured and public vehicles or vessels are not only safe and inspected,

**[04:29:14.600] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005982)  
but also

**[04:29:16.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005992)  
insured accordingly.

**[04:29:17.880] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005993)  
In my committee report to the MV Trisha Kerstin,

**[04:29:20.640] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005994)  
we made some recommendation to Marina.

**[04:29:22.540] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-005995)  
What has happened to this?

**[04:29:26.830] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005996)  
As to the insurance,

**[04:29:29.930] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005997)  
we have now the draft increase for the passenger coverage.

**[04:29:38.110] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005998)  
But it will be submitted to the board

**[04:29:41.650] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-005999)  
despite wala pa pong nabibigay yung Insurance Commission na ang

**[04:29:47.990] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-006003)  
master policy for this.

**[04:29:51.330] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006004)  
So, you know,

**[04:29:52.510] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006005)  
sa akin, you have to understand,

**[04:29:54.250] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006006)  
during the last committee hearing,

**[04:29:55.990] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006007)  
kaya ako sinasabi, kaya ako pinararashan

**[04:29:58.510] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006008)  
because it's rainy season na naman.

**[04:30:00.000] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006009)  
You know, accident waiting to happen na naman eh.

**[04:30:04.460] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006010)  
Gusto na naman ba nangyari yung maganyan tragedy?

**[04:30:06.940] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006011)  
Nowadays, it's so hard to forecast ang weather.

**[04:30:09.980] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006012)  
Pwede ngayon sabihan, clear to go.

**[04:30:11.820] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006013)  
Then in the middle of the voyage,

**[04:30:14.120] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006014)  
biglang mabumagyo.

**[04:30:15.800] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006015)  
We're here to protect the public.

**[04:30:17.820] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006016)  
Same thing with yung

**[04:30:19.080] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006017)  
Yung motorcycle taxis.

**[04:30:20.800] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006018)  
Si, sino ba in charge ngayon?

**[04:30:26.210] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006019)  
LTFRB, Informed on Travel.

**[04:30:28.430] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006020)  
Passengers, manood ka ng news.

**[04:30:30.530] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006021)  
laging tatlo, apat, patay.

**[04:30:32.950] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006022)  
Hindi lang yung

**[04:30:34.430] Rep. Franz S. Pumaren** (asr-006023)  
nagmamaneho
